## Journal objectives and implementation anchors

Objective labels come from the saved local journal export. They identify intent; the implementation transitions below establish graph evidence.

| Objective | Journal path |
| --- | --- |
| When a person dies, the world keeps chugging along without skipping a beat. Eventually, all traces of that person fade into nothing. Long story short, that's why your wheels were towed. Want my opinion? The best way to convince the world you're alive and kicking is to get your wheels back. Hell, maybe it's not the most ambitious goal, but for a minor-league merc like you, at least it's a start. (Primary) | `quests/side_quest/sq025_0_pickup/pickup/00_debug_trigger` |
| Go to the parking lot. (Primary) | `quests/side_quest/sq025_0_pickup/pickup/00a_go_parking_lot` |
| Drive out of the parking garage. (Primary) | `quests/side_quest/sq025_0_pickup/pickup/00b_debug_spawn` |
| Talk to Johnny Silverhand. (Primary) | `quests/side_quest/sq025_0_pickup/pickup/00c_talk_johnny` |
| Get out of the damaged car. (Primary) | `quests/side_quest/sq025_0_pickup/pickup/01_leave_car` |
| Read the message from Delamain. (Primary) | `quests/side_quest/sq025_0_pickup/pickup/02_read_msg` |
| Keep busy while you wait for the car to be repaired. (Primary) | `quests/side_quest/sq025_0_pickup/pickup/03_wait` |

Text is joined from screenplay option IDs to the embedded localization store. An option-level predicate is only one layer of eligibility; parent graph routes can impose additional gates.

World NodeRef definitions/streaming owners, full actor and animation closure, numeric TweakDB names, spoken subtitle/VO payload joins and installed/save behavior have not been validated by this structural pass. Raw direct references, actor bindings, choices, event locstrings and community entries remain in the evidence shards.

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.

## Connected paths and supporting bindings

763 repeated typed/socket path signatures in this selected graph context; 82 distinct direct resource identities; 23 embedded graph handoffs. The full occurrence lists, concrete parameters, exact source pointers and external edges are retained in the checked evidence. Shared forward dependencies can serve several quests; these are context counts.

NodeRef uses: 728; TweakDB binding uses: 67. Uses retain exact pointers in resource evidence. A global alias alone is not a resolved streaming owner; numeric records are not guessed. See [all-quest analysis](../all-quest-findings.md) for extraction rules and proposed contracts.

## Scene performance events

Section event data retains actor/animation/audio bindings and authored time fields, with raw pointers. Events in different sections are not treated as one chronological timeline: parent routing, interruption and playback policy still apply. Animation/voice payloads and installed timing are not validated by this extraction.

| --- | --- | --- |

Query full events with `tools/query_sidequest_evidence.py --root reference/quests/deep-dives/all-quests --quest <exact title or journal path> --events`; add `--resource`, `--graph scene --node <id>` or `--event-type <substring>` to narrow the output. Scene-global screenplay and interruption data remain in the performance shards.
